[E15 MAIN RESULT id=WyxTbrpE] {"id": "page_qa_runbook", "space_id": "space_ENG", "parent_id": "page_eng_home", "title": "QA Regression Runbook", "body": "<p>Manual QA regression pass run against every release candidate before we submit a build for review. Owned by QA (Vincent Parker); anyone cutting a build can run it, but a green matrix here is a gate on the <ac:link><ri:page ri:space-key=\"OPS\" ri:content-title=\"Release Checklist &amp; Store Submission\"/><ac:link-body>Release Checklist &amp; Store Submission</ac:link-body></ac:link>. Scope covers Domino Delights (internal codename GoD, \"Game of Dominoes\"), Zombie Match 3D (listed as \"Puzzles vs Zombies\" until the July 2025 rename). Budget a few hours per title - the first five chapters alone run over an hour; if something fails, file the bug and block the release, don't hand-wave it.</p><ac:structured-macro ac:name=\"note\"><ac:rich-text-body><p><strong>Test the UPDATE path, not just a fresh install.</strong> The June 2025 progression losses were on <strong>Domino Delights</strong>, and nobody ever reproduced them - the leading hypothesis was the update path, which is why we test it. Every candidate must be installed <em>over</em> the last two shipped versions and checked for save/progression loss before we ship. If we only test fresh installs we will miss this entire class of bug again.</p></ac:rich-text-body></ac:structured-macro><h2>Recurring bug classes to always check</h2><p>These four have all bitten us in production. Treat them as non-negotiable checks on every pass, on real devices, not just the emulator.</p><ul><li><strong>Font / asset download stall on splash.</strong> In May 2025 Domino Delights froze at roughly 22% on the splash screen while pulling remote fonts and assets (ENG-2346, build 0.2.197). Watch the loading bar all the way to gameplay; test on a throttled/slow connection and with the app cache cleared.</li><li><strong>Lives &amp; scheduler regeneration.</strong> Confirm lives tick back up on the timer, refill correctly after device clock changes, and that offline time is credited without over-granting.</li><li><strong>Quest population &amp; reset.</strong> New quests populate on a fresh session, complete correctly, and roll over at the daily/weekly reset without duplicating or leaving empty slots.</li><li><strong>Save / progression integrity.</strong> Level, coins and unlocks survive an update, a force-quit and a re-launch. This is the one the June 2025 wipe belongs to.</li></ul><h2>Regression matrix</h2><p>Run each row on at least one iOS and one Android device. Both update rows are mandatory - N-1 and N-2 are the last two shipped store versions.</p><table><tbody><tr><th>Area</th><th>Fresh install</th><th>Update from N-1</th><th>Update from N-2</th><th>Notes</th></tr><tr><td>Splash / asset load</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Watch for the ~22% stall (May 2025 Domino Delights)</td></tr><tr><td>Save / progression</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>June 2025 wipe never reproduced in-house; update path is the leading hypothesis</td></tr><tr><td>Lives &amp; scheduler</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Test with device clock changes</td></tr><tr><td>Quests populate / reset</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Check daily + weekly rollover</td></tr><tr><td>Purchase / restore</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Pass</td><td>Sandbox account. NOTE: entitlements do <strong>not</strong> restore on reinstall and receipts are not validated server-side - see the per-title IAP row below. Check the purchase grants client-side, not that it restores.</td></tr></tbody></table><h3>If a check fails</h3><ul><li>File the bug with device, OS version, source build and repro steps, and link it here.</li><li>A failure in any UPDATE column blocks the release - fresh-install-only regressions do not pass the gate.</li><li>Player-facing symptoms (lost progress, stuck splash, missing lives) should be mirrored to the <ac:link><ri:page ri:space-key=\"OPS\" ri:co
...[TRUNCATED 4371 chars]...
nt-gen iPhone, latest OS</td><td>iPhone SE-class, oldest supported OS</td><td>iPad (one gen)</td></tr>\n<tr><td>Android</td><td>current-gen Samsung / Pixel</td><td>low-RAM device, oldest supported OS</td><td>one Android tablet</td></tr></tbody></table>\n<h3>Sign-off</h3><p>QA posts the completed matrix in <code>#god-bugs (Domino Delights) or #zombie-bugs (ZM3D)</code> and links it from the release ticket. A build without a green matrix should not get submitted. In practice Friday is the usual release day and builds have shipped without a completed pass - QA has asked for a Friday freeze but it has not been adopted.</p>", "body_format": "storage", "status": "current", "version": 2, "author_id": "user_EMPLOYEE_0025", "created_at": "2024-04-15T10:11:23Z", "updated_at": "2025-08-04T10:00:00Z"}

[E31 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__gdrive_read_file id=kyZoqUD2] {"fileId": "f_632c44650df44fe252ee59"}
[E32 MAIN RESULT id=kyZoqUD2] {"mimeType": "text/markdown", "text": "# ZM submission rules\nLeonard, Sep 23\n\nAfter the orange juice one on Sunday I want the gate tighter, not just the Bug view in Linear.\n\nScope: every ZM ticket that's still open (any state but Done, Canceled or Duplicate), whatever team or label (ART or DES tickets with ZM in the title count too), plus everything that moved to Done after the last gate's builds were cut (Android #127 / iOS #40 on Sep 22). Release tickets, ZOM-359 and ZOM-475 stay out, that work is separate. From the next gate on, tickets filed while the gate is running get a row too, including the ones you file yourself.\n\nA ticket counts as fixed when Vincent or Brian saw it working on the candidate builds, or on prod if the fix is server side. A Done ticket for a bug nobody has re-checked yet also counts as fixed if its fix is actually in the candidate builds (or live on prod), noted as not re-checked. Done in Linear or a dev saying fixed isn't enough by itself, and if the latest check on the candidates still shows the bug, call it on how bad it is whatever the ticket says.\n\nBlocker = crash, freeze, player stuck or can't finish a level (like the orange juice one), lost progress / coins / lives (infinite-lives time included) / boosters / inventory items, or a purchase that doesn't deliver. Any other bug a player can see is a known issue, even if it doesn't change play, visual and sound glitches included. Art/VFX requests, requests for design, balance or tuning changes, analytics, tools, backend endpoints and new features aren't bugs, no call. The same goes for a ticket that only asks for new or updated art, animation, VFX, text style or UI layout (replacing an outdated model or icon with its new version included), even with a Bug label and even once it's Done, and for a ticket that doesn't say what goes wrong.\n\nBlockers go on the release ticket. If in doubt ask me before the go/no-go, not after.\n", "id": "f_632c44650df44fe252ee59", "name": "ZM submission rules.docx"}

[E33 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__gdrive_read_file id=DVoPCbHe] {"fileId": "f_a038a576ca8db6955fae95"}
[E34 MAIN RESULT id=DVoPCbHe] {"mimeType": "text/markdown", "text": "# How ZM submission builds are cut\nArthur, Sep 24\n\nSo nobody has to guess when gating:\n\n- Since the hotfix train ended on Sep 3, every store submission (the Match3D Android Submission and Match 3D iOS Submission jobs) is cut straight from main. The auth branch submission jobs are sign-in test builds, not store candidates.\n- A submission has exactly what was on main when its job started (the Started line in #builds) and nothing that landed after, even if the build finishes an hour later. Don't go by the Changes block either: the iOS posts only ever print the newest change, Android prints the whole list.\n- Backend is separate: match3d-backend changes reach players only when Douglas deploys the backend to prod, by hand (see the CI/CD page, CI stops at the build). Merged is not live.\n", "id": "f_a038a576ca8db6955fae95", "name": "How ZM submission builds are cut.docx"}

[E35 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__gdrive_read_file id=qtn7iHcf] {"fileId": "f_5e40215b7d4d24b0455412"}
[E36 MAIN RESULT id=qtn7iHcf] {"mimeType": "text/markdown", "text": "# Update-path run - iOS #50 / Android #135\nBrian Foster, Sep 30 2025 (after the fresh-install pass)\n\nUpdate rows from the QA regression runbook. Fresh-install rows are in my regression matrix sheet.\n\nDevice | OS | Installed from store | Updated to | Result | Notes\niPhone 13 | iOS 18.6 | N-1 | iOS Submission #50 | FAIL | After the update the save came back at level 1 with 500 coins; the account was on level 212 with 18,400 coins before. Relaunching didn't bring it back.\niPhone 13 | iOS 18.6 | N-2 | iOS Submission #50 | Pass | Level, coins and unlocks kept.\nPixel 6 | Android 14 | N-1 | Android Submission #135 | Pass | Level, coins and unlocks kept.\nPixel 6 | Android 14 | N-2 | Android Submission #135 | Pass | Level, coins and unlocks kept.\n\nRepro for the iOS row: install the N-1 build from the App Store, play past level 200, install TestFlight 50 over it, launch.\n", "id": "f_5e40215b7d4d24b0455412", "name": "Update-path run - iOS #50 / Android #135.docx"}

[E37 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__gdrive_read_file id=mkdnJ7mY] {"fileId": "f_b91db376f65604b8602657"}

[E390 SUB USER-TEXT] This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
The parent agent asked for these two calls on every ticket:
- mcp__hg__linear_get_issue (id, includeRelations true)
- mcp__hg__linear_list_comments (issueId, orderBy createdAt, limit 50)

Constraint (verbatim): "Read-only; do not modify anything."

Each ticket gets a compact block:
- "ID | title | status | labels | completedAt"
- What goes wrong, in 1-2 sentences taken from the description. If there is no description, say "no description of what goes wrong". Note when the ticket is really an art, VFX, UI-layout, design, analytics, feature or tuning request.
- Every comment, verbatim or near-verbatim, with the author's email and UTC timestamp. Keep anything that mentions builds #135/#50/#127/#40, TestFlight, prod, PRs, commits, deploys, migrations, re-test results, "fixed" or "still repro".

[E473 SUB RESULT id=3puB3WSx] {"id": "ZOM-535", "uuid": null, "identifier": "ZOM-535", "number": 535, "title": "Fast matching might target an already-dead zombie", "description": "Here is a video demonstrating the difference between 75 CPM vs. 120 CPM simulation on the exact same level, because faster matchers end up with a lot of inefficiencies where their item doesn't deal damage since it's flying towards an already killed monster. As a result they end with the nuke. Meanwhile the 75 CPM average player ends up with quite a bit of items left. It's too big of a gap and we should really find ways to tighten all this up\n\n[[CREDIT_CARD_NUMBER_6354].3602723.mp4]([URL_52842])\n\nHere is a slow-mo video of what happens. \n\n[persistent health bar - Made with Clipchamp (1).mp4]([URL_52841])", "priority": {"value": 1, "name": "Urgent"}, "estimate": null, "url": "https://linear.app/harmonygames/issue/ZOM-535/fast-matching-might-target-an-already-dead-zombie", "gitBranchName": "vincentparker/zom-535-fast-matching-might-target-an-already-dead-zombie", "createdAt": "2025-09-08T12:51:11Z", "updatedAt": "2025-09-30T21:42:00Z", "archivedAt": null, "completedAt": null, "startedAt": null, "canceledAt": null, "dueDate": null, "status": "Todo", "statusType": "unstarted", "labels": ["Bug"], "links": [], "attachments": [], "createdBy": "vincent.parker@harmonygames.co", "createdById": "usr_65c76e0b87aa7e9b", "assignee": "EMPLOYEE_0032_NAME", "assigneeId": "usr_EMPLOYEE_0032", "team": "Zombie Match 3D", "teamId": "team_ZOM", "timeInStatusMinutes": 32773, "isArchived": false, "stateHistory": [], "relations": {"blocks": [], "blockedBy": [], "relatedTo": [], "duplicateOf": null}}

[E717 MAIN CALL mcp__hg__linear_create_issue id=SDxhAfzF] {"team": "Zombie Match 3D", "title": "iOS #50 installed over the N-1 store build resets the save to level 1", "priority": 1, "labels": ["Bug"], "relatedTo": ["ZOM-570"], "description": "Found by the update-path rows of the QA Regression Runbook during the ZOM-570 gate (Android Submission #135 / iOS Submission #50).\n\n**What goes wrong**\nUpdating from the N-1 App Store build to iOS Submission #50 loses the player's progress. Before the update the account was on level 212 with 18,400 coins. After the update the save came back at level 1 with 500 coins, and relaunching didn't restore it.\n\n**Device / build**\niPhone 13, iOS 18.6. Installed N-1 from the App Store, then updated to iOS Submission #50 (TestFlight 50).\n\n**Repro**\n1. Install the N-1 build from the App Store and play past level 200.\n2. Install TestFlight 50 (iOS Submission #50) over it.\n3. Launch: the save shows level 1 / 500 coins.\n\n**Other update rows (same run)**\n- iOS N-2 → #50: pass (level, coins and unlocks kept)\n- Android N-1 → #135: pass\n- Android N-2 → #135: pass\nFresh-install rows passed on both platforms (Brian's regression matrix).\n\nSource: Brian Foster's \"Update-path run - iOS #50 / Android #135\" doc (Drive). Per the runbook, a failure in any UPDATE column blocks the release. Lost progress/coins also makes this a blocker under the ZM submission rules."}

The QA call on Android #135 / iOS #50 is **no-go**, with 14 blockers. Brian replied "matrix green" in the thread, but that covered only fresh installs; his own update-path run shows a failure.

**Sheet:** I added a tab "Sep 30 - Android #135 / iOS #50" to your ZM3D Release Gate sheet. It has 133 rows: 14 blockers, 23 known issues, 10 fixed and 86 no call. Each row has a build status and a link to the evidence. The scope follows Leonard's rules: every open ZM ticket, plus everything moved to Done after the Sep 22 builds (started 12:30Z).

**New ticket:** I filed **ZOM-581**. Updating iOS #50 over the previous store version reset the save to level 1 with 500 coins; before the update it was level 212 with 18,400 coins (iPhone 13, from Brian's update-path doc). The runbook says a failure on the update path blocks the release. The update from the version before that, and both Android updates, passed. Nothing else from the runbook checks was unfiled.

**Release ticket:** ZOM-570's description now lists all 14 blockers by key, using the existing ticket wherever there is one. The ones you might not expect:
- **ZOM-581:** the update-path progress loss above.
- **ZOM-435:** marked Done, but Leonard got stuck on the trophy path again on #135 (his Trello comment on the tech-issues card).
- **ZOM-569:** marked Done, but the fix landed on main at 21:36Z on Sep 30. Both candidates were started around 18:44Z, so the fix isn't in them.
- **ZOM-567:** the backend fix (#36) is merged, but Arthur says the data load it depends on hasn't been run on prod. He's holding it until after the store push, so chest rewards still don't land in the inventory.
- **DES-313:** level 214 can't be finished (a Design-team ticket, but ZM).
- **ZOM-449, 483, 560, 554, 546, 573, 576, 578:** re-tested or newly filed on the candidates, each a freeze, crash, stuck player, or lost lives/items/purchase.
- **ZOM-559:** a Samsung freeze on the victory screen, last seen on #133. Nobody has re-checked it on #135, so it's called on severity.

**Slack:** the QA call is posted as a reply in Arthur's candidates thread in #zombie-bugs, as Leonard asked on ZOM-570.

Three calls you may want to look at before Friday:
- **ZOM-563** (Teams lives not working on prod): I called it a known issue. It could be a blocker if players are actually losing lives.
- **ZOM-427** (Android app icon too zoomed in): I called it a known issue, though it may fall under Leonard's no-call rule for icon/art updates.
- **ZOM-473**: counted as fixed only because it was closed before the candidates were cut. Nobody has re-checked it on these builds.

Leonard asked to be consulted before the go/no-go if anything is in doubt.
